- `/auth`: GET main.py, POST api/routers/accounts.py

- `/parent/tasks/{task_id}/cancel`: POST .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, POST .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, POST api/routers/platform.py
- `/parent/tasks/{task_id}`: DELETE .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, GET .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, DELETE .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, GET .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, DELETE api/routers/platform.py, GET api/routers/platform.py

- `/student/tasks/{task_id}/submit`: POST .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, POST .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, POST api/routers/platform.py
- `/student/rewards/{reward_id}/buy`: POST .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, POST .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, POST api/routers/platform.py
- `/chat/history`: GET .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, DELETE .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, GET .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, DELETE .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, GET api/routers/platform.py, DELETE api/routers/platform.py
- `/chat/messages`: POST .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, POST .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, POST api/routers/platform.py
- `/parent/tasks`: POST .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, GET .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, POST .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, GET .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, POST api/routers/platform.py, GET api/routers/platform.py

- `/parent/children/{student_id}/tasks`: GET .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, GET .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, GET api/routers/platform.py
- `/parent/rewards`: GET .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, POST .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, GET .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, POST .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, GET api/routers/platform.py, POST api/routers/platform.py
- `/parent/rewards/{reward_id}`: PUT .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, DELETE .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, PUT .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, DELETE .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, PUT api/routers/platform.py, DELETE api/routers/platform.py
- `/admin/overview`: GET .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, GET .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, GET api/routers/platform.py
- `/admin/books`: GET .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, POST .tz20_v4_backup/platform.py, GET .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, POST .tz20_v2_backup/api/routers/platform.py, GET api/routers/platform.py, POST api/routers/platform.py
